-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+    const idx_t last_row = static_cast<idx_t>(last) * static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local; ++local_idx) {
+        const idx_t global_idx = global_begin + static_cast<idx_t>(local_idx);
+        if (global_idx == 0) {
+            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        }
+        if (global_idx == static_cast<idx_t>(last)) {
+            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        }
+        if (global_idx == last_row) {
+            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        }
+        if (global_idx == last_row + static_cast<idx_t>(last)) {
+            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
+    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
+    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
+        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
+            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
+            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return bounds;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
+    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
+    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
+}
+
+// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
+// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
+// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
+// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
+// contiguous partition in practice.
+void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
+    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
+    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
+
+    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
+    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
+    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
+
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
+            } else {
+                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
+                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
+                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
+    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
+    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
+    world.recv_peers.clear();
+    world.halo_energy.clear();
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (requests.empty()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.halo_energy.size();
+        world.recv_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, requests.size()});
+        for (const idx_t global_idx : requests) {
+            halo_slot_by_global.emplace(global_idx,
+                                        static_cast<idx_t>(world.halo_energy.size()));
+            world.halo_energy.push_back(0.0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Convert remote global IDs in each connection into direct halo indices.
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor < world.global_begin || global_neighbor >= global_end) {
+                const auto slot = halo_slot_by_global.find(global_neighbor);
+                if (slot == halo_slot_by_global.end()) {
+                    MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                    return;
+                }
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    n_local + slot->second;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    world.interior_elements.clear();
+    world.boundary_elements.clear();
+    world.interior_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    world.boundary_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        bool has_remote_neighbor = false;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            if (world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] >= n_local) {
+                has_remote_neighbor = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        (has_remote_neighbor ? world.boundary_elements : world.interior_elements).push_back(local_idx);
+    }
+
+    // Exchange request lists.  This is a setup-only all-to-all; the hot loop
+    // uses only the sparse peer lists created below.
+    std::vector<int> send_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const std::size_t count = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].size();
+        if (count > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+            return;
+        }
+        send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Alltoall(send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);
+
+    std::vector<int> send_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    int send_total = 0;
+    int recv_total = 0;
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = send_total;
+        recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = recv_total;
+        send_total += send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        recv_total += recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<idx_t> send_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(send_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        std::copy(requests.begin(), requests.end(),
+                  send_ids.begin() + send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+    std::vector<idx_t> received_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    MPI_Alltoallv(send_ids.empty() ? nullptr : send_ids.data(), send_counts.data(),
+                  send_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,
+                  received_ids.empty() ? nullptr : received_ids.data(), recv_counts.data(),
+                  recv_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);
+
+    world.send_peers.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const int count = recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.send_local_indices.size();
+        world.send_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, static_cast<std::size_t>(count)});
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            const idx_t global_idx = received_ids[
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] + i)];
+            if (global_idx < world.global_begin || global_idx >= global_end) {
+                MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                return;
+            }
+            world.send_local_indices.push_back(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(global_idx - world.global_begin));
+        }
+    }
+    world.send_buffer.resize(world.send_local_indices.size());
+}
+

                         val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {

 // Run simulation for n_iters iterations

+void updateElements(World& world, const std::vector<std::size_t>& indices) {
+    const std::size_t n_local = world.elements_static.size();
+    const ElementDynamic* current = world.elements_dynamic.data();
+    ElementDynamic* next = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();
+
+    for (const std::size_t i : indices) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = current[i];
+        const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
+
+        // Start with external flow
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+
+        // Add flux from all connected elements.  Local references index the
+        // current state directly; remote references index the received halo.
+        const std::size_t ref_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
+            const idx_t ref = world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(j)];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy =
+                ref < n_local ? current[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref)].current_energy
+                              : world.halo_energy[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref - n_local)];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *
+                          mat.transfer_coeff * elem_static.connected_flux[j] * 0.25;
+        }
+
+        // Update element state
+        next[i].current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+

+uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t global_begin) {

+std::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world,
+                                          const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                                          const int rank, const int n_ranks,
+                                          MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_elems = bounds.back();
+    if (n_elems > static_cast<idx_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const idx_t count = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer) + 1] -
+                            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] =
+            static_cast<int>(bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+
+    static_assert(sizeof(ElementDynamic) == 2 * sizeof(val_t));
+    MPI_Datatype dynamic_type;
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &dynamic_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&dynamic_type);
+
+    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        all_elements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_elems));
+    }
+    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.empty() ? nullptr : world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)], dynamic_type,
+                rank == 0 && !all_elements.empty() ? all_elements.data() : nullptr,
+                counts.data(), displacements.data(), dynamic_type, 0, comm);
+
+    MPI_Type_free(&dynamic_type);
+    return all_elements;
+}

+std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
+    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
+    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
+        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
+            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
+            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return bounds;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
+    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
+    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
+}
+
+// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
+// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
+// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
+// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
+// contiguous partition in practice.
+void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
+    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
+    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
+
+    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
+    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
+    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
+
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
+            } else {
+                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
+                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
+                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
+    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
+    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
+    world.recv_peers.clear();
+    world.halo_energy.clear();
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (requests.empty()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.halo_energy.size();
+        world.recv_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, requests.size()});
+        for (const idx_t global_idx : requests) {
+            halo_slot_by_global.emplace(global_idx,
+                                        static_cast<idx_t>(world.halo_energy.size()));
+            world.halo_energy.push_back(0.0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Convert remote global IDs in each connection into direct halo indices.
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor < world.global_begin || global_neighbor >= global_end) {
+                const auto slot = halo_slot_by_global.find(global_neighbor);
+                if (slot == halo_slot_by_global.end()) {
+                    MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                    return;
+                }
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    n_local + slot->second;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    world.interior_elements.clear();
+    world.boundary_elements.clear();
+    world.interior_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    world.boundary_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        bool has_remote_neighbor = false;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            if (world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] >= n_local) {
+                has_remote_neighbor = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        (has_remote_neighbor ? world.boundary_elements : world.interior_elements).push_back(local_idx);
+    }
+
+    // Exchange request lists.  This is a setup-only all-to-all; the hot loop
+    // uses only the sparse peer lists created below.
+    std::vector<int> send_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const std::size_t count = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].size();
+        if (count > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+            return;
+        }
+        send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Alltoall(send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);
+
+    std::vector<int> send_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    int send_total = 0;
+    int recv_total = 0;
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = send_total;
+        recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = recv_total;
+        send_total += send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        recv_total += recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<idx_t> send_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(send_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        std::copy(requests.begin(), requests.end(),
+                  send_ids.begin() + send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+    std::vector<idx_t> received_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    MPI_Alltoallv(send_ids.empty() ? nullptr : send_ids.data(), send_counts.data(),
+                  send_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,
+                  received_ids.empty() ? nullptr : received_ids.data(), recv_counts.data(),
+                  recv_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);
+
+    world.send_peers.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const int count = recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.send_local_indices.size();
+        world.send_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, static_cast<std::size_t>(count)});
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            const idx_t global_idx = received_ids[
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] + i)];
+            if (global_idx < world.global_begin || global_idx >= global_end) {
+                MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                return;
+            }
+            world.send_local_indices.push_back(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(global_idx - world.global_begin));
+        }
+    }
+    world.send_buffer.resize(world.send_local_indices.size());
+}

+void updateElements(World& world, const std::vector<std::size_t>& indices) {
+    const std::size_t n_local = world.elements_static.size();
+    const ElementDynamic* current = world.elements_dynamic.data();
+    ElementDynamic* next = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();
+
+    for (const std::size_t i : indices) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = current[i];
+        const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
+
+        // Start with external flow
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+
+        // Add flux from all connected elements.  Local references index the
+        // current state directly; remote references index the received halo.
+        const std::size_t ref_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
+            const idx_t ref = world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(j)];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy =
+                ref < n_local ? current[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref)].current_energy
+                              : world.halo_energy[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref - n_local)];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *
+                          mat.transfer_coeff * elem_static.connected_flux[j] * 0.25;
+        }
+
+        // Update element state
+        next[i].current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+    }
+}

+uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t global_begin) {

+        hash ^= (*e_ptr + global_idx) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;

 }
 
+std::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world,
+                                          const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                                          const int rank, const int n_ranks,
+                                          MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_elems = bounds.back();
+    if (n_elems > static_cast<idx_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const idx_t count = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer) + 1] -
+                            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] =
+            static_cast<int>(bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+
+    static_assert(sizeof(ElementDynamic) == 2 * sizeof(val_t));
+    MPI_Datatype dynamic_type;
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &dynamic_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&dynamic_type);
+
+    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        all_elements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_elems));
+    }
+    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.empty() ? nullptr : world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)], dynamic_type,
+                rank == 0 && !all_elements.empty() ? all_elements.data() : nullptr,
+                counts.data(), displacements.data(), dynamic_type, 0, comm);
+
+    MPI_Type_free(&dynamic_type);
+    return all_elements;
+}

+std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
+    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
+    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
+        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
+            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
+            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return bounds;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
+    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
+    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
+}
+
+// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
+// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
+// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
+// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
+// contiguous partition in practice.
+void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
+    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
+    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
+
+    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
+    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
+    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
+
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
+            } else {
+                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
+                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
+                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
+    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
+    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
+    world.recv_peers.clear();
+    world.halo_energy.clear();
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (requests.empty()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.halo_energy.size();
+        world.recv_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, requests.size()});
+        for (const idx_t global_idx : requests) {
+            halo_slot_by_global.emplace(global_idx,
+                                        static_cast<idx_t>(world.halo_energy.size()));
+            world.halo_energy.push_back(0.0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Convert remote global IDs in each connection into direct halo indices.
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor < world.global_begin || global_neighbor >= global_end) {
+                const auto slot = halo_slot_by_global.find(global_neighbor);
+                if (slot == halo_slot_by_global.end()) {
+                    MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                    return;
+                }
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    n_local + slot->second;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    world.interior_elements.clear();
+    world.boundary_elements.clear();
+    world.interior_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    world.boundary_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        bool has_remote_neighbor = false;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            if (world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] >= n_local) {
+                has_remote_neighbor = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        (has_remote_neighbor ? world.boundary_elements : world.interior_elements).push_back(local_idx);
+    }
+
+    // Exchange request lists.  This is a setup-only all-to-all; the hot loop
+    // uses only the sparse peer lists created below.
+    std::vector<int> send_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const std::size_t count = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].size();
+        if (count > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+            return;
+        }
+        send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Alltoall(send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);
+
+    std::vector<int> send_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    int send_total = 0;
+    int recv_total = 0;
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = send_total;
+        recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = recv_total;
+        send_total += send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        recv_total += recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<idx_t> send_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(send_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        std::copy(requests.begin(), requests.end(),
+                  send_ids.begin() + send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+    std::vector<idx_t> received_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    MPI_Alltoallv(send_ids.empty() ? nullptr : send_ids.data(), send_counts.data(),
+                  send_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,
+                  received_ids.empty() ? nullptr : received_ids.data(), recv_counts.data(),
+                  recv_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);
+
+    world.send_peers.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const int count = recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.send_local_indices.size();
+        world.send_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, static_cast<std::size_t>(count)});
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            const idx_t global_idx = received_ids[
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] + i)];
+            if (global_idx < world.global_begin || global_idx >= global_end) {
+                MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                return;
+            }
+            world.send_local_indices.push_back(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(global_idx - world.global_begin));
+        }
+    }
+    world.send_buffer.resize(world.send_local_indices.size());
+}

+void updateElements(World& world, const std::vector<std::size_t>& indices) {
+    const std::size_t n_local = world.elements_static.size();
+    const ElementDynamic* current = world.elements_dynamic.data();
+    ElementDynamic* next = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();
+
+    for (const std::size_t i : indices) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = current[i];
+        const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
+
+        // Start with external flow
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+
+        // Add flux from all connected elements.  Local references index the
+        // current state directly; remote references index the received halo.
+        const std::size_t ref_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
+            const idx_t ref = world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(j)];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy =
+                ref < n_local ? current[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref)].current_energy
+                              : world.halo_energy[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref - n_local)];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *
+                          mat.transfer_coeff * elem_static.connected_flux[j] * 0.25;
+        }
+
+        // Update element state
+        next[i].current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+    }
+}

 // Compute a simple hash of the results for verification

+uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t global_begin) {

+std::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world,
+                                          const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                                          const int rank, const int n_ranks,
+                                          MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_elems = bounds.back();
+    if (n_elems > static_cast<idx_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const idx_t count = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer) + 1] -
+                            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] =
+            static_cast<int>(bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+
+    static_assert(sizeof(ElementDynamic) == 2 * sizeof(val_t));
+    MPI_Datatype dynamic_type;
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &dynamic_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&dynamic_type);
+
+    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        all_elements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_elems));
+    }
+    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.empty() ? nullptr : world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)], dynamic_type,
+                rank == 0 && !all_elements.empty() ? all_elements.data() : nullptr,
+                counts.data(), displacements.data(), dynamic_type, 0, comm);
+
+    MPI_Type_free(&dynamic_type);
+    return all_elements;
+}

    81	
    82	// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
    83	// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
    84	void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root,
    85	                   const idx_t global_begin, const idx_t global_end) {
    86	    // Initialize materials
    87	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
    88	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
    89	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
    90	
    91	    world.global_begin = global_begin;
    92	    const std::size_t n_local = static_cast<std::size_t>(global_end - global_begin);
    93	
    94	    // Allocate elements
    95	    world.elements_static.resize(n_local);
    96	    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_local);
    97	    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_local);
    98	
    99	    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
   100	    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n_local; ++i) {
   101	        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
   102	        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
   103	        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
   104	        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
   108	    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local; ++local_idx) {
   109	        const idx_t global_idx = global_begin + static_cast<idx_t>(local_idx);
   110	        const idx_t x = global_idx / static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);
   111	        const idx_t y = global_idx % static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);
   112	        ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
   113	
   114	        // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
   115	        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
   116	
   117	        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
   118	            // Check if neighbor is within bounds.  The signed checks avoid
   119	            // unsigned underflow for the first row and column.
   120	            const int64_t signed_nx = static_cast<int64_t>(x) + offsets[n][0];
   121	            const int64_t signed_ny = static_cast<int64_t>(y) + offsets[n][1];
   122	            if (signed_nx >= 0 && signed_nx < n_elems_root &&
   123	                signed_ny >= 0 && signed_ny < n_elems_root) {
   124	                    const idx_t neighbor_idx =
   125	                        static_cast<idx_t>(signed_nx) * static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root) +
   126	                        static_cast<idx_t>(signed_ny);
   127	                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
   128	                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
   129	                    elem.num_connections++;
   130	            }
   131	        }
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
   135	    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
   136	    const idx_t last_row = static_cast<idx_t>(last) * static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);
   137	    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local; ++local_idx) {
   138	        const idx_t global_idx = global_begin + static_cast<idx_t>(local_idx);
   139	        if (global_idx == 0) {
   140	            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   141	        }
   142	        if (global_idx == static_cast<idx_t>(last)) {
   143	            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   144	        }
   145	        if (global_idx == last_row) {
   146	            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
   147	        }
   148	        if (global_idx == last_row + static_cast<idx_t>(last)) {
   149	            world.elements_static[local_idx].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
   150	        }
   151	    }
   152	}
   153	
   154	std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
   155	    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
   156	    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
   157	    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
   158	
   159	    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
   160	        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
   161	            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
   162	            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   163	    }
   164	    return bounds;
   165	}
   166	
   167	int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
   168	    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
   169	    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
   170	}
   171	
   172	// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
   173	// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
   174	// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
   175	// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
   176	// contiguous partition in practice.
   177	void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
   178	                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
   179	    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
   180	    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
   181	    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
   182	
   183	    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
   184	    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
   185	    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
   186	    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
   187	
   188	    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
   189	        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
   190	        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
   191	        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
   192	            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
   193	            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
   194	                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
   195	                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
   196	            } else {
   197	                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
   198	                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
   199	                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
   200	                }
   201	            }
   202	        }
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
   206	    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
   207	    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
   208	    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
   209	    world.recv_peers.clear();
   210	    world.halo_energy.clear();
   340	        // Start with external flow
   341	        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
   342	
   343	        // Add flux from all connected elements.  Local references index the
   344	        // current state directly; remote references index the received halo.
   345	        const std::size_t ref_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
   346	        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
   347	            const idx_t ref = world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(j)];
   348	            const val_t neighbor_energy =
   349	                ref < n_local ? current[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref)].current_energy
   350	                              : world.halo_energy[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref - n_local)];
   351	            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *
   352	                          mat.transfer_coeff * elem_static.connected_flux[j] * 0.25;
   353	        }
   354	
   355	        // Update element state
   356	        next[i].current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
   357	        next[i].total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
   358	    }
   359	}
   360	
   361	void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, MPI_Comm comm) {
   362	    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
   363	    requests.reserve(world.recv_peers.size() + world.send_peers.size());
   364	
   365	    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   366	        requests.clear();
   367	
   368	        // Post receives first, then pack and post sends.  Each peer has one
   369	        // message in each direction, so the fixed tag is safe after the
   370	        // wait at the end of this iteration.
   371	        for (const World::PeerPlan& peer : world.recv_peers) {
   372	            MPI_Request request;
   373	            MPI_Irecv(world.halo_energy.data() + peer.offset,
   374	                      static_cast<int>(peer.count), MPI_DOUBLE, peer.rank, 0, comm, &request);
   375	            requests.push_back(request);
   376	        }
   377	
   378	        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < world.send_local_indices.size(); ++i) {
   379	            world.send_buffer[i] =
   380	                world.elements_dynamic[world.send_local_indices[i]].current_energy;
   381	        }
   382	        for (const World::PeerPlan& peer : world.send_peers) {
   383	            MPI_Request request;
   384	            MPI_Isend(world.send_buffer.data() + peer.offset,
   385	                      static_cast<int>(peer.count), MPI_DOUBLE, peer.rank, 0, comm, &request);
   386	            requests.push_back(request);
   387	        }
   388	
   389	        // Interior elements do not depend on the halo and overlap useful
   390	        // computation with network progress.
   391	        updateElements(world, world.interior_elements);
   392	
   393	        if (!requests.empty()) {
   394	            MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   395	        }
   396	        updateElements(world, world.boundary_elements);
   397	
   398	        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
   399	    }
   400	}
   401	
   402	// Validate simulation results
   403	bool validateResults(const World& world) {
   404	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
   405	    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
   406	    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   407	    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   408	    
   409	    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
   410	        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
   411	        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
   412	        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
   413	        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
   414	    }
   415	    
   416	    printf("Validation results:\n");
   417	    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   418	    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   419	    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   420	    
   421	    // Check for numerical issues
   422	    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
   423	    
   424	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   425	        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   426	        return false;
   427	    }
   428	    
   429	    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
   430	        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   431	        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
   432	    }
   433	    
   434	    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   435	        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   436	        return false;
   437	    }
   438	    
   439	    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   440	        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   441	        return false;
   442	    }
   443	    
   444	    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   445	    return true;
   446	}
   447	
   448	// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
   449	uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t global_begin) {
   450	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   451	    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
   452	        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
   453	        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
   454	        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
   455	        const uint64_t global_idx = global_begin + static_cast<idx_t>(i);
   456	        hash ^= (*e_ptr + global_idx) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   457	        hash ^= (*f_ptr + global_idx) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
   458	    }
   459	    return hash;
   460	}
   461	
   462	std::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world,
   463	                                          const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
   464	                                          const int rank, const int n_ranks,
   465	                                          MPI_Comm comm) {
   466	    const idx_t n_elems = bounds.back();
   467	    if (n_elems > static_cast<idx_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   468	        MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
   469	        return {};
   470	    }
   471	
   472	    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
   473	    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
   474	    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
   475	        const idx_t count = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer) + 1] -
   476	                            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
   477	        counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
   478	        displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] =
   479	            static_cast<int>(bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
   480	    }
   481	
   482	    static_assert(sizeof(ElementDynamic) == 2 * sizeof(val_t));
   483	    MPI_Datatype dynamic_type;
   484	    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &dynamic_type);
   485	    MPI_Type_commit(&dynamic_type);
   486	
   487	    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
   488	    if (rank == 0) {
   489	        all_elements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_elems));
   490	    }
   491	    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.empty() ? nullptr : world.elements_dynamic.data(),
   492	                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)], dynamic_type,
   493	                rank == 0 && !all_elements.empty() ? all_elements.data() : nullptr,
   494	                counts.data(), displacements.data(), dynamic_type, 0, comm);
   495	
   496	    MPI_Type_free(&dynamic_type);
   497	    return all_elements;
   498	}
   499	
   500	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   501	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   502	    printf("Options:\n");
   503	    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
   504	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
   505	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   506	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   507	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   508	}
   509	
   510	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   511	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   512	
   513	    int rank = 0;
   514	    int n_ranks = 1;
   515	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   516	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &n_ranks);
   517	
   518	    int n_elems_root = 512;
   519	    int n_iters = 10;
   520	    bool validate = false;
   521	    bool printResults = false;
   522	
   523	    // Parse command line arguments
   524	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   525	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   526	            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);
   527	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   528	            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);
   529	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   530	            validate = true;
   531	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   532	            printResults = true;
   533	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   534	            if (rank == 0) {
   535	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   536	            }
   537	            MPI_Finalize();
   538	            return 0;
   539	        } else {
   540	            if (rank == 0) {
   541	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   542	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   543	            }
   544	            MPI_Finalize();
   545	            return 1;
   546	        }
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    if (n_elems_root <= 0) {
   550	        if (rank == 0) {
   551	            printf("Grid size must be positive\n");
   552	        }
   553	        MPI_Finalize();
   554	        return 1;
   555	    }
   556	
   557	    const idx_t n_elems = static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root) *
   558	                          static_cast<idx_t>(n_elems_root);
   559	    const std::vector<idx_t> bounds = makePartitionBounds(n_elems, n_ranks);
   560	    const idx_t global_begin = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)];
   561	    const idx_t global_end = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1];
   562	
   563	    if (rank == 0) {
   564	        printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
   565	        printf("============================================\n");
   566	        printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %" PRIu64 " elements\n",
   567	               n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);
   568	        printf("Iterations: %d\n", n_iters);
   569	        printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", n_ranks);
   570	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   571	        printf("\n");
   572	    }
   573	
   574	    // Build the unstructured mesh
   575	    if (rank == 0) {
   576	        printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
   577	    }
   578	    World world;
   579	    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root, global_begin, global_end);
   580	    setupCommunication(world, bounds, n_ranks, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   581	
   582	    // Calculate memory usage
   583	    const size_t static_mem = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems) * sizeof(ElementStatic);
   584	    const size_t dynamic_mem = static_cast<size_t>(n_elems) * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;
   585	    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;
   586	    if (rank == 0) {
   587	        printf("Memory usage (aggregate): %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n",
   588	               total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   589	               static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   590	               dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   591	        printf("\n");
   592	
   593	        printf("Running simulation...\n");
   594	    }
   595	
   596	    // Run simulation
   597	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   598	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   599	    runSimulation(world, n_iters, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   600	    const double local_elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   601	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   602	    MPI_Reduce(&local_elapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   603	
   604	    // Compute hash for verification
   605	    const uint64_t local_hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic, world.global_begin);
   606	    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
   607	    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   608	
   609	    if (rank == 0) {
   610	        const long duration_ms = std::max(1L, static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0));
   611	        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration_ms);
   612	
   613	        // Calculate performance metrics
   614	        const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);
   615	        const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;
   616	        const double giga_elems_per_sec =
   617	            (static_cast<double>(n_measured_iters) * static_cast<double>(n_elems)) /
   618	            (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;
   619	
   620	        // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)
   621	        const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;
   622	
   623	        printf("Performance:\n");
   624	        printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iter);
   625	        printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elems_per_sec);
   626	        printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   627	        printf("  Result hash: %016" PRIX64 "\n", result_hash);
   628	        printf("\n");
   629	    }
   630	
   631	    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
   632	    // Print results for external validation
   633	    if (printResults || validate) {
   634	        all_elements = gatherDynamic(world, bounds, rank, n_ranks, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   635	    }
   636	    if (rank == 0 && printResults) {
   637	        std::vector<double> energyData;
   638	        energyData.reserve(all_elements.size());
   639	        for (const auto& elem : all_elements) {
   640	            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);
   641	        }
   642	        print_results(energyData, "ElementEnergy");
   643	    }
   644	
   645	    // Validation
   646	    int valid = 1;
   647	    if (validate) {
   648	        if (rank == 0) {
   649	            World gathered_world;
   650	            gathered_world.elements_dynamic = std::move(all_elements);
   651	            valid = validateResults(gathered_world) ? 1 : 0;
   652	        }
   653	    }
   654	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   655	
   656	    MPI_Finalize();
   657	    return valid == 1 ? 0 : 1;
   658	}

+std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
+    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
+    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
+        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
+            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
+            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return bounds;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
+    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
+    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
+}
+
+// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
+// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
+// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
+// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
+// contiguous partition in practice.
+void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
+    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
+    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
+
+    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
+    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
+    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
+
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
+            } else {
+                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
+                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
+                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
+    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
+    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
+    world.recv_peers.clear();
+    world.halo_energy.clear();
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (requests.empty()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.halo_energy.size();
+        world.recv_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, requests.size()});
+        for (const idx_t global_idx : requests) {
+            halo_slot_by_global.emplace(global_idx,
+                                        static_cast<idx_t>(world.halo_energy.size()));
+            world.halo_energy.push_back(0.0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Convert remote global IDs in each connection into direct halo indices.
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor < world.global_begin || global_neighbor >= global_end) {
+                const auto slot = halo_slot_by_global.find(global_neighbor);
+                if (slot == halo_slot_by_global.end()) {
+                    MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                    return;
+                }
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    n_local + slot->second;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    world.interior_elements.clear();
+    world.boundary_elements.clear();
+    world.interior_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    world.boundary_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        bool has_remote_neighbor = false;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            if (world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] >= n_local) {
+                has_remote_neighbor = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        (has_remote_neighbor ? world.boundary_elements : world.interior_elements).push_back(local_idx);
+    }
+
+    // Exchange request lists.  This is a setup-only all-to-all; the hot loop
+    // uses only the sparse peer lists created below.
+    std::vector<int> send_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const std::size_t count = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].size();
+        if (count > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+            return;
+        }
+        send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Alltoall(send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);
+
+    std::vector<int> send_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    int send_total = 0;
+    int recv_total = 0;
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = send_total;
+        recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = recv_total;
+        send_total += send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        recv_total += recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<idx_t> send_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(send_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        std::copy(requests.begin(), requests.end(),
+                  send_ids.begin() + send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+    std::vector<idx_t> received_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    MPI_Alltoallv(send_ids.empty() ? nullptr : send_ids.data(), send_counts.data(),
+                  send_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,
+                  received_ids.empty() ? nullptr : received_ids.data(), recv_counts.data(),
+                  recv_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);
+
+    world.send_peers.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const int count = recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.send_local_indices.size();
+        world.send_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, static_cast<std::size_t>(count)});
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            const idx_t global_idx = received_ids[
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] + i)];
+            if (global_idx < world.global_begin || global_idx >= global_end) {
+                MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                return;
+            }
+            world.send_local_indices.push_back(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(global_idx - world.global_begin));
+        }
+    }
+    world.send_buffer.resize(world.send_local_indices.size());
+}

+void updateElements(World& world, const std::vector<std::size_t>& indices) {
+    const std::size_t n_local = world.elements_static.size();
+    const ElementDynamic* current = world.elements_dynamic.data();
+    ElementDynamic* next = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();
+
+    for (const std::size_t i : indices) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = current[i];
+        const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
+
+        // Start with external flow
+        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
+
+        // Add flux from all connected elements.  Local references index the
+        // current state directly; remote references index the received halo.
+        const std::size_t ref_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
+            const idx_t ref = world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(j)];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy =
+                ref < n_local ? current[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref)].current_energy
+                              : world.halo_energy[static_cast<std::size_t>(ref - n_local)];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - elem_dyn.current_energy) *
+                          mat.transfer_coeff * elem_static.connected_flux[j] * 0.25;
+        }
+
+        // Update element state
+        next[i].current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+

 // Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
-uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
+uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements, const idx_t global_begin) {
     uint64_t hash = 0;
     for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
         // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
         const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);

+        hash ^= (*e_ptr + global_idx) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;

+std::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world,
+                                          const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                                          const int rank, const int n_ranks,
+                                          MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_elems = bounds.back();
+    if (n_elems > static_cast<idx_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const idx_t count = bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer) + 1] -
+                            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] =
+            static_cast<int>(bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+
+    static_assert(sizeof(ElementDynamic) == 2 * sizeof(val_t));
+    MPI_Datatype dynamic_type;
+    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &dynamic_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&dynamic_type);
+
+    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all_elements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        all_elements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_elems));
+    }
+    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.empty() ? nullptr : world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)], dynamic_type,
+                rank == 0 && !all_elements.empty() ? all_elements.data() : nullptr,
+                counts.data(), displacements.data(), dynamic_type, 0, comm);
+
+    MPI_Type_free(&dynamic_type);
+    return all_elements;
+}

+
+std::vector<idx_t> makePartitionBounds(const idx_t n_elems, const int n_ranks) {
+    std::vector<idx_t> bounds(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks) + 1, 0);
+    const idx_t base = n_elems / static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+    const idx_t remainder = n_elems % static_cast<idx_t>(n_ranks);
+
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < n_ranks; ++rank) {
+        bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) + 1] =
+            bounds[static_cast<std::size_t>(rank)] + base +
+            (static_cast<idx_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return bounds;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const idx_t global_idx, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds) {
+    const auto it = std::upper_bound(bounds.begin(), bounds.end(), global_idx);
+    return static_cast<int>(it - bounds.begin()) - 1;
+}
+
+// Construct the one-time halo exchange plan.  Each rank advertises the
+// global IDs it needs; owners receive those requests and return the matching
+// local values every iteration.  This also works for arbitrary connectivity,
+// even though the benchmark's generated mesh has at most two peer ranks per
+// contiguous partition in practice.
+void setupCommunication(World& world, const std::vector<idx_t>& bounds,
+                        const int n_ranks, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const idx_t n_local = static_cast<idx_t>(world.elements_static.size());
+    const idx_t global_end = world.global_begin + n_local;
+    const std::size_t n_local_size = static_cast<std::size_t>(n_local);
+
+    world.connection_refs.assign(n_local_size * MAX_CONNECTIONS, 0);
+    std::vector<std::vector<idx_t>> requests_by_owner(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks));
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, unsigned char> remote_seen;
+    remote_seen.reserve(n_local_size * 2 + 1);
+
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor >= world.global_begin && global_neighbor < global_end) {
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    global_neighbor - world.global_begin;
+            } else {
+                const int peer = ownerOf(global_neighbor, bounds);
+                if (remote_seen.emplace(global_neighbor, 0).second) {
+                    requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].push_back(global_neighbor);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Assign contiguous halo slots in peer order.  The grouped layout lets
+    // every receive write directly into the halo array without unpacking.
+    std::unordered_map<idx_t, idx_t> halo_slot_by_global;
+    halo_slot_by_global.reserve(remote_seen.size() + 1);
+    world.recv_peers.clear();
+    world.halo_energy.clear();
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (requests.empty()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.halo_energy.size();
+        world.recv_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, requests.size()});
+        for (const idx_t global_idx : requests) {
+            halo_slot_by_global.emplace(global_idx,
+                                        static_cast<idx_t>(world.halo_energy.size()));
+            world.halo_energy.push_back(0.0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Convert remote global IDs in each connection into direct halo indices.
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            const idx_t global_neighbor = elem.connected_idx[connection];
+            if (global_neighbor < world.global_begin || global_neighbor >= global_end) {
+                const auto slot = halo_slot_by_global.find(global_neighbor);
+                if (slot == halo_slot_by_global.end()) {
+                    MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                    return;
+                }
+                world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] =
+                    n_local + slot->second;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    world.interior_elements.clear();
+    world.boundary_elements.clear();
+    world.interior_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    world.boundary_elements.reserve(n_local_size);
+    for (std::size_t local_idx = 0; local_idx < n_local_size; ++local_idx) {
+        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
+        const std::size_t ref_base = local_idx * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+        bool has_remote_neighbor = false;
+        for (idx_t connection = 0; connection < elem.num_connections; ++connection) {
+            if (world.connection_refs[ref_base + static_cast<std::size_t>(connection)] >= n_local) {
+                has_remote_neighbor = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        (has_remote_neighbor ? world.boundary_elements : world.interior_elements).push_back(local_idx);
+    }
+
+    // Exchange request lists.  This is a setup-only all-to-all; the hot loop
+    // uses only the sparse peer lists created below.
+    std::vector<int> send_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_counts(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const std::size_t count = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)].size();
+        if (count > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+            return;
+        }
+        send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Alltoall(send_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, recv_counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, comm);
+
+    std::vector<int> send_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    std::vector<int> recv_displacements(static_cast<std::size_t>(n_ranks), 0);
+    int send_total = 0;
+    int recv_total = 0;
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = send_total;
+        recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] = recv_total;
+        send_total += send_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        recv_total += recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<idx_t> send_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(send_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const auto& requests = requests_by_owner[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        std::copy(requests.begin(), requests.end(),
+                  send_ids.begin() + send_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)]);
+    }
+    std::vector<idx_t> received_ids(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    MPI_Alltoallv(send_ids.empty() ? nullptr : send_ids.data(), send_counts.data(),
+                  send_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T,
+                  received_ids.empty() ? nullptr : received_ids.data(), recv_counts.data(),
+                  recv_displacements.data(), MPI_UINT64_T, comm);
+
+    world.send_peers.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.clear();
+    world.send_local_indices.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_total));
+    for (int peer = 0; peer < n_ranks; ++peer) {
+        const int count = recv_counts[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)];
+        if (count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        const std::size_t offset = world.send_local_indices.size();
+        world.send_peers.push_back(World::PeerPlan{peer, offset, static_cast<std::size_t>(count)});
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            const idx_t global_idx = received_ids[
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(recv_displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(peer)] + i)];
+            if (global_idx < world.global_begin || global_idx >= global_end) {
+                MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
+                return;
+            }
+            world.send_local_indices.push_back(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(global_idx - world.global_begin));
+        }
+    }
+    world.send_buffer.resize(world.send_local_indices.size());

 // Run simulation for n_iters iterations
